JsonTree is the instance this scope reached, but the seam is the hook's adoption boundary and no single file shows it — a probe of its own.
- `B06` (verifier) — packages/ui/src/__benchmarks__/kanban.bench.tsx appeared in a filename-only search for `KanbanCard`. It was NOT opened, per §5.1; no benchmark and no sweep evidence was read in this pass.
- `B05` (verifier) — `resolveAlertPresentation`'s doccomment claims a symmetry the function does not have. `packages/ui/src/components/alert/alert.tsx:94` says it resolves "from severity, falling back to the explicit `color`/`icon` props", but `alert.tsx:105` makes `color` a fallback while `alert.tsx:107` makes `icon` an override. The comment is accurate on one axis and wrong on the other. Adjacent to B05-C02, on the axis that claim did not name.
- `B05` (verifier) — Alert's public @remarks and its gate test disagree about when the announcer fires. `alert.tsx:156-157` promises re-announcement "on appear" and grounds it in WCAG 4.1.3 and "a live region inserted together with its text" — which is the mount case — while `alert.tsx:205-206` and `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/alert.test.tsx:142` both scope it to closed→open. The summary line names the case the implementation excludes. A doc-versus-gate seam on an accessibility claim; I propose no document change (§5.5).
- `B05` (verifier) — The Alert announcer's one live path is not reachable from the demo that would exercise it. `packages/ui/src/docs/demos/components/alert.tsx:15-35` (`ClosableExample`) returns a `<Button>` in place of the `<Alert>` when dismissed, so Reset remounts the alert rather than reopening a mounted one — and a remount is the silent path. The same shape appears at `:37-58`. A demo-level observation about which paths the shipped surfaces can reach.
- `B05` (verifier) — Drawer and Sheet dress the same `PanelHandle` primitive by two incompatible positioning models, and the primitive states neither. `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/drawer.ts:84-85` puts the area in flow (`flex.row`, `'w-full shrink-0 items-center justify-center'`, `layout.flush`); `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/sheet.ts:95-99` makes it absolute and full-height. `packages/ui/src/primitives/panel/panel-handle.tsx:30-38` says the two "differ in where the grip sits and what it looks like and in nothing else" and delegates the box entirely to the kata. A cross-component seam no single file shows; the sheet half of it is B05-C06.
- `B05` (verifier) — `SheetHandle` renders as the first child inside `PanelProviders` (`packages/ui/src/components/sheet/sheet.tsx:240-242`) with no counterpart to the drawer's `layout.flush`, which `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/panel/layout.ts:29` exists to cancel the slot gap above a handle. Whether the sheet needs the same cancellation depends on how S3 resolves the handle's box, so it is adjacent to B05-C06 rather than part of it.
- `B05` (verifier) — `SheetProps` accepts `width` on every value of `side`, while `sheet.tsx:38-39` states in prose that it applies to two of them. The same shape recurs on `handle`, which is accepted for all four sides. A discriminated `SheetProps` would make both B05-C05 and B05-C06 unrepresentable rather than merely fixed — a design seam that outlives whichever way Q1 and Q2 go.
- `B08` (verifier) — `useA11yAnnouncements` never records a null message — `packages/ui/src/hooks/a11y/use-a11y-announcements.ts:35` returns before `:37` updates `previous.current` — so every caller in the package that lets its status go quiet and then return to the same string loses the second announcement. B08-C03 is one instance; the seam is shared and crosses components, so it belongs to its own probe rather than to this scope.
- `B08` (verifier) — `packages/ui/src/components/pdf-viewer/use-pdf-viewer-document.ts:27` holds a module-level `sharedWorker` that is created once and never released (`:65` — " sharedWorker = pdfjs.PDFWorker.create({ port })"). That is process-global state of the kind the 2026-08-02-TEST-SUITE-AUDIT open row enumerates for other singletons, and the pdf-viewer cache ships a `resetDocumentCache()` for its own map but nothing for the worker. Adjacent, not judged.
- `B08` (verifier) — `packages/ui/src/components/pdf-viewer/pdf-viewer-viewport.tsx` stamps `data-slot="pdf-viewer-page-frame"` on two different elements — the loaded frame at `:62` and the loading `<output>` placeholder at `:88`. The branches are mutually exclusive, so it is not the collision B08-C09 names, but one anchor names two structurally different things and a browser test already waits on it (`packages/ui/src/__tests__/browser/pdf-viewer-highlight-layer.test.tsx:104`).
- `B08` (verifier) — `PdfViewerZoomControls` and `PdfViewerDocumentActions` are the only two interactive `.tsx` files in `packages/ui/src/components/pdf-viewer/` without `'use client'` — 25 of the directory's 31 source files carry it, including every other sub-component leaf. No failure is reachable today (B08-C07, B08-C08 refuted), but the directory's pattern is otherwise exact, so the pair reads as an omission rather than a decision. A sweep, not a fix.
- `B08` (verifier) — `packages/ui/src/components/pdf-viewer/pdf-viewer-document-cache.ts:115-127` runs `evict()` only from the unsubscribe path and from `settle`, and skips every held or loading entry (`:121`). The TSDoc concedes the shape ("a run with every entry held frees nothing") and rests the bound on "the app mounts one scan at a time", which is an assumption about consumers rather than a property of the cache. Adjacent to G3's file, not judged.
- `B07` (verifier) — packages/ui/src/docs/engine/app.tsx:53-55 asserts the recovery B07-C08 shows cannot happen — "// A warm prefetch; a failed chunk fetch (offline, post-deploy 404) is // harmless here — CodeBlock re-invokes loadShiki on render and shows its // plain fallback — so swallow the rejection rather than leaking it." The re-invocation is real (code-block.tsx:111) but returns the memoised rejection, so the shell's stated reason for swallowing is unsound. The file is outside the B07 list; route it back to a sweep over packages/ui/src/docs/engine.
- `B07` (verifier) — packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/card.test.tsx:96-98 documents a rule the recipe does not carry — "// The header-collapse rule only matches a CardBody next sibling; it's // present in the class list but its `:has()` guard won't fire here" — and then asserts nothing about it. A test comment that describes an absent selector is a second-order staleness the 2026-09-12 doc audit's comment-accuracy category has not reached in the test tree.
- `B07` (verifier) — packages/ui/src/modules/grid/grid-row-manager.tsx:108 carries a consumer working around the Card header gap — "edge — a CardHeader would add a bottom gap for a body that isn't here." A module avoiding a slot because of its projected spacing is a cross-component signal on the B07-C05 seam and belongs with whatever answer Q2 takes.
- `B07` (verifier) — packages/ui/src/components/stepper/stepper-step.tsx:137 is the only caller of `mountsEveryPanel` repo-wide (`packages/ui/src/primitives/mount/index.ts:2` exports it). Every other family with a `mount` policy and a trigger-to-panel reference — Tabs is the obvious candidate — may carry the same gap B07-C02 and B07-C03 record. A cross-component probe, not a single-file finding.
- `B07` (verifier) — packages/ui/src/components/avatar/avatar.tsx:43 `const initialsHidden = !!src || !alt` combines with the default at :29 `alt = '',` so an avatar given `src` and no `alt` renders `<img … alt="">` (:67) with the initials svg suppressed, leaving the avatar with no accessible name at all. Adjacent to B07-C11 but a different input, and no claim in this batch names it.
- `B07` (verifier) — packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/avatar.ts mixes projection depths inside one object — :33 `ring: '*:ring-2 *:ring-white dark:*:ring-zinc-900',` at direct-child depth against :46-48 `'**:data-[slot=avatar]:size-7'` … at descendant depth — with only the size row's comment (:39-44) explaining the choice. Whether other kata projections mix depths against the packages/ui/REFERENCE.md:28 rule ("Direct-child and exact-depth selectors keep nested instances independent") is a sweep question over recipes/kata, which the 2026-09-12 doc audit lists as unread.
- `B09` (verifier) — `useSortableSensors` calls `useSensor(KeyboardSensor, …)` unconditionally at packages/ui/src/hooks/use-sortable-sensors.ts:83 and then discards it at :87 when `keyboard` is false, so Kanban and List build a descriptor every render that nothing reads. Adjacent to S1's file; not judged.
- `B09` (verifier) — `usePanelFit`'s effect lists `dragged` among its dependencies (packages/ui/src/hooks/use-panel-fit.ts:168), so every gesture start and end tears the observer down and re-declares `let rested: BorderBox | null = null` at :110. The first content change after a drag ends therefore adopts its height with no travel — the same symptom C06 described, reached by a route C06 did not name. Adjacent; not judged.
- `B09` (verifier) — `useFloatingUI` accepts `role: 'tooltip'` (packages/ui/src/hooks/use-floating-ui.ts:401) but calls `useEscapeLayer` with no `layered` (:442-445), where the sibling `useFloatingDisclosure` unlayers exactly that role (:153). The two hooks disagree about one role that only one of them can serve correctly — a cross-hook seam, not a defect at any call site today.
- `B09` (verifier) — The global jsdom double at packages/ui/src/__tests__/mocks/floating-ui.ts:112-116 renders `FloatingPortal` children synchronously, where the resolved package mounts them a commit later. Every deferred-mount defect in a portalled surface — Popover, Menu, Tooltip, DatePicker, and the panel family — is invisible to the unit suite by construction. This is a cross-component test-fidelity seam and belongs in its own probe, not in a row.
- `B10` (verifier) — packages/ui/src/providers/glass/glass.tsx carries no `'use client'` (line 1 is `import type { ReactNode } from 'react'`) yet renders a client Context provider directly at :20 `<GlassContext value={true}>`, while packages/ui/src/providers/glass/context.ts:1 does carry the directive. This is the same unmarked tier boundary C04 names, in a second provider, and in a stronger form (a rendered provider rather than a hook). The scan that would catch it walks only `components/` and `modules/`: component-boundary.test.ts:92 `for (const dir of [componentsDir, modulesDir])`.
- `B10` (verifier) — `useMountHold`'s `defer` flips at runtime at two shipped call sites without any `mount` change: packages/ui/src/modules/grid/use-grid-reveal-hold.ts:67 `useMountHold(expanded, 'always', { defer: !reducedMotion })` and packages/ui/src/components/collapse/collapse-panel.tsx:38 `useMountHold(open, mount, { defer: animate !== false })`. A false-to-true `defer` flip on an inactive held panel reaches the same one-way latch C07 names (mount.ts:99,104,116) from the opposite direction — the panel is hidden, then un-hides. Adjacent to C07, not judged.
- `B10` (verifier) — packages/ui/src/providers/toast/toast.tsx:113 `resetRemaining(data.duration ?? duration)` runs before the persist check at :115 `if (!data.persist) startTimer()`, so raising a persistent toast rewrites the shared countdown that the next `startTimer()` will arm, even though the persistent toast itself never expires.
- `B10` (verifier) — packages/ui/src/layouts/dashboard.tsx renders `filters` into the tree twice — :46 `<Stack>{filters}</Stack>` inside the desktop aside and :61 the same inside `DrawerBody`. The aside is hidden by CSS (`max-lg:hidden`, :45) rather than unmounted, so while the mobile drawer is open a stateful filter subtree is mounted in both places at once. A cross-file seam (layout plus Drawer presence), not a single-file finding.
- `B11` (verifier) — packages/ui/src/utilities/scrollbar-press.ts:41 — the LTR arm `event.offsetX > target.clientWidth` also counts the right border strip as gutter, the mirror of the RTL border over-count judged under B11-C11, but on the opposite edge and outside that claim's trigger.
- `B11` (verifier) — packages/ui/src/core/validation-attrs.ts:35-36 `return severity && ATTRS[severity]` returns `undefined` for an absent severity, and the declared `ValidationAttrs` union only admits it because `InvalidAttrs` carries the `undefined` arm (packages/ui/src/core/invalid-attrs.ts:3 `export type InvalidAttrs = typeof INVALID | undefined`). The warning and success arms alone would not type-check, so the union's soundness rides on the error arm's shape.
- `B11` (verifier) — packages/ui/src/hooks/a11y/use-a11y-has-tabbable.ts:42-47 observes `attributeFilter: ['tabindex', 'disabled', 'href']`, but `FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR` also keys off an input's `type`; a `type` flip between `hidden` and `text` on an existing node changes the answer and is not observed.
- `B11` (verifier) — packages/ui/src/utilities/print-frame.ts:42-46 — `cleanup` is wrapped in `once`, so if `iframe.remove()` ever threw, the `window` `focus` listener registered at :54 would stay registered and `cleanup` would run again on the next focus; a cross-file seam between the `once` semantics judged under B11-C10 and the print path's listener bookkeeping.
- `B11` (verifier) — The imperative `announce` (packages/ui/src/core/announcer.ts:52) and the declarative `useA11yAnnouncements` (packages/ui/src/hooks/a11y/use-a11y-announcements.ts:39 `announce(message, { assertive })`) write through the same two module-scope regions with no coordination, so a component narrating a changing value and a one-off toast share one node and can overwrite each other mid-announcement. This is a cross-component seam no single file shows.
